# Changelog

Notable changes to the SDK. Changes of behaviour are called out.

## 0.3.0 (2026-11-18)

Compared with 0.2.2.

### Breaking changes

Code using these must change.

- `price`: the section `price` is no longer generated: Catalog and pricing data, not an API to manage resources. It is large and changes with every offer. The Go SDK never shipped it.
- `storage`: `listNashas`: the `items/` response's property `type` changed from `string` to `object` for status `200` (GET /v1/storage/nasha)
- `vrackServices`: `GetVrackServiceEligibleDedicatedServerInterfaces400Response.class`: removed
- `vrackServices`: `GetVrackServiceEligibleDedicatedServerInterfaces400Response.message`: removed
- `vrackServices`: `GetVrackServiceEligibleDedicatedServerInterfaces400Response`: removed
- `vrackServices`: `GetVrackServiceEligibleDedicatedServerInterfaces404Response.class`: removed
- `vrackServices`: `GetVrackServiceEligibleDedicatedServerInterfaces404Response.message`: removed
- `vrackServices`: `GetVrackServiceEligibleDedicatedServerInterfaces404Response`: removed

### Removed by the API

The API no longer serves these, so their TypeScript names are gone.

- `hostingprivateDatabase`: `MYSQL_8_0` ("mysql_8.0", of `HostingPrivateDatabaseAvailableVersionEnum`)
- `hostingprivateDatabase`: `MYSQL_8_0` ("mysql_8.0", of `HostingPrivateDatabaseVersionEnum`)
- `publicCloud`: 16 operations, 62 types, listed below
- `storage`: 7 operations, 2 types, listed below
- `vrackServices`: `allVrackServiceEligibleDedicatedServerInterfaces` (GET /v2/vrackServices/resource/{vrackServicesId}/eligibleDedicatedServerInterfaces)
- `vrackServices`: `getResourceEligibleBgpAllowedPrefixes` (GET /v2/vrackServices/resource/{vrackServicesId}/eligibleBgpAllowedPrefixes)
- `vrackServices`: `VrackServicesEligibleBgpAllowedPrefixes`
- `vrackServices`: `VrackServicesEligibleDedicatedServerInterfaceItem`
- `vrackServices`: `VrackServicesEligibleDedicatedServerInterface`

### Changed

No change to the TypeScript API, but the API behaves differently.

- `storage`: `confirmTerminationNasha`: added `#/components/schemas/service.TerminationFutureUseEnum` to the `futureUse` request property `allOf` list (POST /v1/storage/nasha/{serviceName}/confirmTermination)
- `storage`: `confirmTerminationNasha`: added `#/components/schemas/service.TerminationReasonEnum` to the `reason` request property `allOf` list (POST /v1/storage/nasha/{serviceName}/confirmTermination)

### SDK core

- `dryRun` client option: reads are sent, any other call throws a `DryRunError` with the URL,
  headers and body it would have sent. No token is fetched for it. Like the Go SDK's
  `WithDryRun`.
- `beforeRequest` and `afterRequest` client options: hooks that run once per call, to log it
  or to refuse it. A refused call throws `HookRefusedError`. The after hooks see every call,
  with its status, query ID, error, duration and attempts. Like the Go SDK's
  `WithBeforeRequest` and `WithAfterRequest`.

## 0.2.1 (2026-11-18)

## 0.2.0 (2026-11-17)

First release.

### SDK core

- First release of the TypeScript SDK, generated by sdkgen from the OVHcloud API's OAS3 specs.
- OAuth2 service accounts (client credentials), from `OVH_CLIENT_ID`, `OVH_CLIENT_SECRET` and
  `OVH_REGION`, or your own token source.
- One property per API section on the client: `client.vrack`. Each section is also a subpath
  of the package (`@ovhcloud/sdk/api/vrack`), for smaller bundles with `@ovhcloud/sdk/core`.
- Types of the JSON the API sends and takes, with its own property names. Open enums.
- Typed errors for each common status: `NotFoundError`, `ForbiddenError`... An error names
  the operation and, on a 401 or 403, the IAM actions it needs.
- Async iterators over v2 lists paginated by cursor (`allX`), and over v1 lists of IDs.
- `waitReady`, `waitTask`, `waitGone` and `wait` for asynchronous work.
- `readOnly` and `allowedOperations` client options, checked before anything is sent.
- Retries, off by default, with the same limits as the Go SDK.
- v2 requests send `X-Schemas-Version`: the contract the SDK was generated from, whatever the
  account's own setting.
- Request bodies take `<Type>Writable`, the type without its read-only fields, when the type
  has some. Field docs say which fields are read-only, and give the date and int64 formats.
- Raw calls: `client.get<T>(...)` and the other raw methods take the type of their result
  (not checked at run time). `path(template, params)` builds a path and escapes its values.
- A path without `/v1/` or `/v2/` (`/me`, `/1.0/me`) is refused with `InvalidRequestError`,
  with the path to use.
- `RequestTimeoutError`, a `TransportError`, for a request that took more than its timeout.
  The client's `timeout` also bounds the token request.
- `retrySleep` client option, to skip the waits between retries in tests.
- `ApiError` has `bodyText` and `toJSON()`, so `JSON.stringify(err)` gives its fields.
- Small programs in `examples/`, and the docs (`llms.txt`, `AGENTS.md`, `index/`) exported by
  the package: `require.resolve("@ovhcloud/sdk/llms.txt")`.
- No runtime dependency: `fetch` only. Runs on Node.js 22.12 or later, Deno and Bun. CommonJS
  programs can `require` it. Tested on Node.js 22, 24 and 26.
